Nvidia’s Stock Price Plummets as Board Is Said to Consider Jensen Huang’s Future as CEOGlobal markets and macro · Chips and AI hardware · 12 outlets · 08:10 +5 related
NYT Business, NYT Technology, Guardian Business, Euronews Business and 8 more

Nvidia share prices dropped in after-hours trading on Wednesday as the board considered the future of chief executive Jensen Huang. News of the board deliberations caused Nvidia stock to fall more than 8 percent late on Wednesday. Jensen Huang co-founded Nvidia in the year 1993.

The board deliberations followed revelations that Jensen Huang had an affair with an Nvidia executive reporting to him and a woman identified by United States intelligence as a Chinese spy. Nvidia stock is down about a third from its peak at the start of October, representing a decline of more than $1.5 trillion in market value. India headline Consumer Price Index inflation reached 6.09% year-on-year for October 2026. The October inflation print exceeded market consensus estimates of 5.93% and a prior reading of 5.54%. Rural inflation stood at 6.63% for October 2026.

Ola Electric reported second quarter revenue of ₹421 Crore compared to ₹690 Crore year-on-year. Oil India declared an interim dividend of ₹7 per share for shareholders. Fiem Industries reported second quarter revenue of ₹9.37 Billion versus ₹7.15 Billion year-on-year. Sahi ↗

India's retail inflation based on the Consumer Price Index rose to a two-year high of 6.09% in October, exceeding the Reserve Bank of India's upper tolerance limit of 6%. Consumer Food Price Index inflation accelerated to 8.94% in October compared to 7.74% in September, driven by rural food inflation of 9.09% and urban food inflation of 8.68%. The Reserve Bank of India targets inflation at 4% with a tolerance band of 2% to 6%, and the October data is the final retail inflation release before its December 4 policy decision.

Sujan Hajra from Anand Rathi group stated that relief could come from Brent crude easing to around US$92/bbl, ample foodgrain stocks, and government interventions, while weak monsoon and El Niño risks prolong pressures. ICRA expects year-on-year inflation in the food and beverages segment to remain elevated above 8.0% in November 2026 due to pressure from milk, sugar, and onion prices. Aditi Nayar, Chief Economist at ICRA, stated that core inflation is expected to inch up in November 2026 due to an unfavourable base effect from GST rationalisation, keeping headline CPI inflation at ~6.1%. CNBC TV18 ↗

India's Consumer Price Index rose to 6.09% year-on-year in October according to provisional data. India's rural prices increased by 6.63% year-on-year in October compared to 5.41% in urban areas. The Consumer Food Price Index in India rose 8.94% year-on-year in October.

Rural food inflation reached 9.09% and urban food inflation stood at 8.68% in October. The market is pricing in a 31% probability of a larger 50 bps move at the next Reserve Bank of India meeting. investingLive ↗

CPI | AkzoNobel-Axalta $25 Billion Deal Hits UK Regulatory RoadblockEurope · 21:47
PYMNTS.com

AkzoNobel and Axalta Coating Systems face a deeper regulatory investigation in Britain regarding their proposed $25 billion merger from the Competition and Markets Authority. The Competition and Markets Authority stated on Wednesday that the merger could reduce competition in the market for automotive repair coatings. The Competition and Markets Authority gave AkzoNobel and Axalta until November 18 to propose measures addressing the regulatory concerns.

The combined company would generate approximately $17 billion in annual revenue across automotive, aerospace, industrial, marine, and decorative paint markets. The Competition and Markets Authority found that the combined entity would hold an estimated 40% to 50% share of the UK supply for vehicle-refinish coatings. AkzoNobel and Axalta plan to address British concerns using a divestiture arrangement negotiated with European Union regulators.

The European Commission conditionally approved the transaction last week after the companies agreed to dispose of European vehicle-refinish coatings assets. AkzoNobel and Axalta maintain their expectation that the transaction will close by early 2027. PYMNTS.com ↗

Despite the sanctions, China Telecom Europe does business with RussiaAsia-Pacific · Telecom and media · 19:59
Paperjam.lu

China Telecom Europe plans to purchase more services in Russia and offset mutual debts to recover €3.8m in provisions for bad debts. China Telecom Europe filed its 2025 annual accounts on 21 October with the Luxembourg Trade and Companies Register. China Telecom Europe set aside a specific provision of £3.19m, equivalent to €3.81m, to cover amounts owed by Russian customers.

China Telecom Europe's Russian exposure accounts for nearly 61% of the €6.28m set aside as a provision for all doubtful debts. Rostelecom is explicitly excluded from China Telecom Europe's provision of €3.81m as the only client in Russia identified in the accounting note. China Telecom Europe reported a turnover of €420.15m in 2025, representing a 33.6% increase year-on-year.

China Telecom Europe's net profit more than doubled, rising from €3.15m to €6.89m. Paperjam.lu ↗

Serrari Group

Volkswagen recorded more than 28,000 sales of new energy vehicles in China in October 2026, which is an increase of nearly 350% compared to a year earlier. The monthly sales record for Volkswagen was supported by newly introduced models like the ID. Aura T6 SUV and the ID. Era 5S sedan. Volkswagen reported that its deliveries in China fell 38.5% in the third quarter compared to the same period in the previous year, while deliveries over the first nine months of 2026 declined 30.2%.

Fully electric vehicles accounted for only 3.5% of Volkswagen's sales in China during the first nine months of 2026, and its Chinese joint operations slipped into losses. Data from the China Passenger Car Association showed that passenger new-energy vehicle retail sales reached 1.109 million units in October, down 13.7% from October 2025. From blood group ID to cycle racks: I asked for little ways to fix broken Britain and the public’s ideas give me hope | Martin LewisEurope · Real estate and infrastructure · 2 outlets · 18:19
Guardian Business, BigGo Finance

Martin Lewis launched the Small Ideas Initiative a month ago to collect practical and low-cost suggestions for improving the United Kingdom. The Small Ideas Initiative has received 42,000 suggestions from the public since its launch. Martin Lewis funded the Small Ideas Initiative, while charities Nesta and Involve are running the project in the background.

Nesta and Involve will filter and publish at least 1,000 of the best suggestions and turn 200 of them into fully researched policy proposals. A cross-party panel ranging from Green to Reform will unanimously agree on the final 50-ish proposals to form a Small Ideas manifesto. The deadline to submit solutions at Small-Ideas.org is 5pm on Thursday 12 November. Guardian Business ↗

US mid-term results unlikely to speed up India trade deal as tariff uncertainties persistIndia · 15:03
BusinessLine Economy

The outcome of the US mid-term elections is unlikely to speed up the proposed India-US interim trade agreement because President Donald Trump retains executive powers to impose tariffs. India wants clarity from the ongoing US investigation under Section 301 on excess industrial capacity before finalising its tariff commitments to protect its exporters against competitors like Bangladesh, Vietnam and Cambodia. India wants safeguards in the proposed trade agreement against the Lindsey Graham Sanctioning Russia and Iran Act, which allows the US President to impose tariffs of up to 100 per cent on the five largest buyers of Russian crude oil and natural gas.

The US Supreme Court issued a February 20 ruling invalidating reciprocal tariffs imposed under the International Emergency Economic Powers Act, which altered the tariff landscape. BusinessLine Economy ↗

Crude oil from northern Iraq smuggled to Iran, AfghanistanMiddle East · 10:25
IraqiNews

An Iraqi intelligence investigation found that crude oil and petroleum products are smuggled from the Kirkuk and Sulaymaniyah fields into Iran. Smugglers use the Bashmaq and Parviz Khan border crossings to move the oil into Iran. Small and medium-sized tanker trucks transport the crude oil and derivatives to the Iranian border.

Once inside Iran, the smuggled oil is processed and mixed with Iranian petroleum products at small refineries. The mixed gasoline is sold using false origin certificates to evade international restrictions, with Afghanistan as the primary destination. Iraqi Prime Minister Ali al-Zaidi established a multi-agency investigating group to confront the oil smuggling networks.

Iraq’s Federal Commission of Integrity launched an investigation targeting the oil smuggling network. IraqiNews ↗

The project is located in the Calama copper belt of the Antofagasta region. The partnership connects copper resources in Chile with Adani's integrated copper smelter complex at Mundra in Gujarat. Adani will provide capital, technology-led exploration and large-scale project execution capabilities.

Codelco will contribute geological expertise and international experience to the partnership. The collaboration follows an earlier memorandum of understanding signed in 2025. Swarajya ↗

The International Energy Agency reported that India avoided over USD 100 billion, or Rs 9.4 lakh crore, in coal and natural gas import costs from 2011 to 2025 due to renewable energy expansion. The International Energy Agency projects that India's cumulative savings on coal and gas imports could reach nearly USD 240 billion, or Rs 22.6 lakh crore, by 2030. The International Energy Agency estimated that annual savings from renewable energy in India could reach around USD 35 billion, or Rs 3.3 lakh crore, by 2030.

India's renewable energy capacity nearly quadrupled over the past decade, crossing 300 GW in 2026. Between March 2014 and September 2026, India added 168 GW of solar capacity, 38 GW of wind capacity, and reached nearly 12 GW of bioenergy capacity. ANI News ↗

India has saved more than USD 100 billion, equivalent to Rs 9.4 lakh crore, on coal and natural gas imports from 2011 to 2025 using renewable energy, as stated by the International Energy Agency. Fertiliser use in India needs a rethink, says Deepak Fertilisers CMD SC MehtaChemicals and agriculture · 14:57 +1 related
India Today

Deepak Fertilisers and Petrochemicals Corporation Ltd CMD SC Mehta stated at the Kisan Tak Bharat Agriculture Conference that India must rethink its fertiliser usage because excessive application harms soil health, crop quality, and farmers' incomes. SC Mehta stated that plants absorb only a small share of applied urea and DAP, while the remaining fertiliser seeps into water sources and harms the environment. SC Mehta pointed out that Indian wheat yields are considerably lower compared to those achieved by China.

SC Mehta suggested that government fertiliser subsidies should be transferred directly to farmers instead of using the existing routing system. Deepak Fertilisers and Petrochemicals Corporation Ltd developed crop-specific fertilisers in Maharashtra by combining primary nutrients with micronutrients and biostimulants. SC Mehta reported that the company's crop-specific formulations for sugarcane, cotton, and onions improved crop yields and quality, including better sugar content in sugarcane, fibre quality in cotton, and size and shelf life in onions. Absurd "Made in India" AI Model Exposed as Repackaged Qwen3.5: Huge Fiasco Uncovered, Even Forcing AI to Claim "Originates from India"14:30
36Kr

Indian AI research company 169Pi officially released a multimodal reasoning model named Alpie Seed 9B on November 8. Alpie Seed 9B features 9 billion parameters, supports text, image, and video input, and includes a 262K context window. Developers analyzing configuration files discovered that Alpie Seed 9B's underlying architecture is directly derived from Alibaba's Qwen3.5-9B model.

The model configuration included an identity policy prompt requiring Alpie Seed 9B to claim it was developed by Indian company 169Pi and comes from India. Independent developer Satyajit sampled and compared all 760 tensors of Alpie Seed and Qwen3.5-9B on November 9, finding that Alpie Seed retains the original architecture. On November 9, 169Pi released a statement admitting that Alpie Seed is developed based on Qwen3.5-9B and that failing to highlight the origin was an oversight.

169Pi used 8 NVIDIA H100 GPUs for GRPO reinforcement learning, completing 3034 training steps and consuming about 3472 GPU hours. 36Kr ↗

Customers without bank accounts can send up to ₹50,000 with cash pay-in service, say RBI guidelinesBanks and finance · 11:50
Hindustan Times Business

The Reserve Bank of India issued domestic money transfer directions for 2026 detailing rules for customers without bank accounts. The Reserve Bank of India guidelines apply to scheduled commercial banks, regional rural banks, cooperative banks, and payment system operators. Under the cash pay-out service, recipients without bank accounts can collect transferred money in cash up to ₹ 10,000 per transaction and ₹ 25,000 monthly.

Under the cash pay-in service, unbanked individuals can deposit up to ₹ 50,000 per transaction into a beneficiary account using national electronic funds transfer. Transfers conducted through business correspondents, automated teller machines, and other channels carry a limit of ₹ 5,000 per transaction and a ₹ 25,000 monthly cap per sender. Senders must register using a verified mobile number and a self-certified officially valid document following the Reserve Bank of India 2025 KYC rules.

The Reserve Bank of India repealed older framework reviews from 2024-25 and guidelines from 2011 with the directions issued on Wednesday. Hindustan Times Business ↗

BEML Ltd will initially focus on tunnel-boring machines with diameters ranging from 3.5 metres to 6.5 metres for urban transit and utility projects. BEML Chairman and Managing Director Shantanu Roy stated that the company intends to build proprietary technology with intellectual property remaining in India and bring on board a global consultant.

Shantanu Roy estimates that India's tunnel-boring machine market could grow by around 12% annually over the next seven to eight years. BEML Ltd estimates that it would require around 22 months to deliver its first tunnel-boring machine after securing a contract. Mint Companies ↗

Monsoon’s first large-scale onset occurs over Northeast India, suggests IIT-Madras, Gauhati University studyChemicals and agriculture · 13:24
Down To Earth

Researchers from the Indian Institute of Technology, Madras and Gauhati University published a study in the Journal of Climate last month indicating the Indian monsoon system first forms over Northeast India around May 15. The study analyzed 84 years of daily rainfall data spanning from 1940 to 2023 utilizing a climate-network approach. Around June 9, the Northeast and peninsular rainfall clusters merge over the peninsula to establish the large-scale monsoon system.

The large-scale rainy season in Northeast India and the southwestern peninsula is estimated by the study to be 20-30 days shorter than the locally defined rainy season. Applying the method to the 2026 southwest monsoon, researchers identified June 22 as the date of large-scale onset over the Indian peninsula. Down To Earth ↗

Ferrexpo halts Ukraine iron ore output after power attacksMetals and mining · 15:27
Mining.com.au

Ferrexpo temporarily stopped production at its Ukrainian iron ore operations after recent attacks disrupted the country's electricity infrastructure. Ferrexpo reported that damage to substations and transmission lines made electricity supplies unstable and insufficient for maintaining production. Ferrexpo stated that its mining and processing assets escaped physical damage during the attacks.

Ferrexpo plans to resume production once commercial electricity supplies are restored, though no specific timetable was given. The recent stoppage marks Ferrexpo's third suspension of operations during 2026 due to power disruptions, limited working capital, and constrained export routes. Ferrexpo recorded commercial production of 780,000 tonnes in the September quarter, which was 19% lower than the 963,000 tonnes produced in the June quarter.

Ferrexpo produced 2.04 million tonnes of pellets in the first nine months of 2026, marking a 27% decrease compared to the same period in 2025. Mining.com.au ↗

France blames Iran for supermarket fridge hacks, seeks EU sanctions09:00
EU Perspectives

The French government accused Tehran of conducting cyberattacks that sabotaged supermarket refrigerators, spoiled food, and caused fires across roughly 700 commercial sites. French investigators identified Shahid Kaveh, a cyber unit linked to Iran’s Revolutionary Guards, as responsible for the attacks. The French Foreign Ministry stated it would summon Iran’s ambassador and propose new European Union sanctions against the individuals and organisations involved.

French prosecutors requested international arrest warrants for several members of the Shahid Kaveh group. France’s cybersecurity agency ANSSI stated that hackers raised refrigeration settings during the night of 11–12 October. French authorities estimated that the resulting food losses amounted to tens of millions of euros after at least 15 fires broke out.

A November wave of refrigeration incidents also affected French blood banks, forcing staff to move medical supplies into other cold storage. EU Perspectives ↗

European survey reveals 900m galaxies – and possibly secrets of ‘dark universe’16:38
Guardian Europe

The European Space Agency launched the Euclid mission in July 2023 to map a third of the sky and create a 3D map of the universe. The Euclid mission's first major data release captures over 900 million galaxies and more than 60 million potential quasars. The newly released data covers approximately 14 percent of the total sky area scheduled to be mapped by the Euclid mission.

Dark energy and dark matter are estimated to comprise about 70 percent and 25 percent of the universe respectively. The Euclid data includes about 15,000 probable cases of strong gravitational lensing, compared to about 1,000 previously known cases. Andy Taylor stated that a further release of data is expected to land next summer. Guardian Europe ↗

Customers without bank accounts can send up to ₹50,000 with cash pay-in service, say RBI guidelinesIndia · Banks and finance · 11:50
Hindustan Times Business

The Reserve Bank of India issued domestic money transfer directions for 2026 detailing rules for customers without bank accounts. The Reserve Bank of India guidelines apply to scheduled commercial banks, regional rural banks, cooperative banks, and payment system operators. Under the cash pay-out service, recipients without bank accounts can collect transferred money in cash up to ₹ 10,000 per transaction and ₹ 25,000 monthly.

Under the cash pay-in service, unbanked individuals can deposit up to ₹ 50,000 per transaction into a beneficiary account using national electronic funds transfer. Transfers conducted through business correspondents, automated teller machines, and other channels carry a limit of ₹ 5,000 per transaction and a ₹ 25,000 monthly cap per sender. Senders must register using a verified mobile number and a self-certified officially valid document following the Reserve Bank of India 2025 KYC rules.

The Reserve Bank of India repealed older framework reviews from 2024-25 and guidelines from 2011 with the directions issued on Wednesday. Hindustan Times Business ↗

Fertiliser use in India needs a rethink, says Deepak Fertilisers CMD SC MehtaIndia · Chemicals and agriculture · 14:57 +1 related
India Today

Deepak Fertilisers and Petrochemicals Corporation Ltd CMD SC Mehta stated at the Kisan Tak Bharat Agriculture Conference that India must rethink its fertiliser usage because excessive application harms soil health, crop quality, and farmers' incomes. SC Mehta stated that plants absorb only a small share of applied urea and DAP, while the remaining fertiliser seeps into water sources and harms the environment. SC Mehta pointed out that Indian wheat yields are considerably lower compared to those achieved by China.

SC Mehta suggested that government fertiliser subsidies should be transferred directly to farmers instead of using the existing routing system. Deepak Fertilisers and Petrochemicals Corporation Ltd developed crop-specific fertilisers in Maharashtra by combining primary nutrients with micronutrients and biostimulants. SC Mehta reported that the company's crop-specific formulations for sugarcane, cotton, and onions improved crop yields and quality, including better sugar content in sugarcane, fibre quality in cotton, and size and shelf life in onions. India Today ↗

Monsoon’s first large-scale onset occurs over Northeast India, suggests IIT-Madras, Gauhati University studyIndia · Chemicals and agriculture · 13:24
Down To Earth

Researchers from the Indian Institute of Technology, Madras and Gauhati University published a study in the Journal of Climate last month indicating the Indian monsoon system first forms over Northeast India around May 15. The study analyzed 84 years of daily rainfall data spanning from 1940 to 2023 utilizing a climate-network approach. Around June 9, the Northeast and peninsular rainfall clusters merge over the peninsula to establish the large-scale monsoon system.

The large-scale rainy season in Northeast India and the southwestern peninsula is estimated by the study to be 20-30 days shorter than the locally defined rainy season. Applying the method to the 2026 southwest monsoon, researchers identified June 22 as the date of large-scale onset over the Indian peninsula. Down To Earth ↗

Swarajya, ANI News, Zee Business

Adani Group's Kutch Copper Ltd signed a memorandum of understanding with Chile's Codelco on November 12, 2026. The memorandum of understanding targets the exploration of the Kaburkaala copper project in northern Chile. The agreement was signed during London Metal Exchange Week 2026 in London.

The project is located in the Calama copper belt of the Antofagasta region. The partnership connects copper resources in Chile with Adani's integrated copper smelter complex at Mundra in Gujarat. Adani will provide capital, technology-led exploration and large-scale project execution capabilities.

Codelco will contribute geological expertise and international experience to the partnership. The collaboration follows an earlier memorandum of understanding signed in 2025. Swarajya ↗

The International Energy Agency reported that India avoided over USD 100 billion, or Rs 9.4 lakh crore, in coal and natural gas import costs from 2011 to 2025 due to renewable energy expansion. The International Energy Agency projects that India's cumulative savings on coal and gas imports could reach nearly USD 240 billion, or Rs 22.6 lakh crore, by 2030. The International Energy Agency estimated that annual savings from renewable energy in India could reach around USD 35 billion, or Rs 3.3 lakh crore, by 2030.

India's renewable energy capacity nearly quadrupled over the past decade, crossing 300 GW in 2026. Between March 2014 and September 2026, India added 168 GW of solar capacity, 38 GW of wind capacity, and reached nearly 12 GW of bioenergy capacity. ANI News ↗

India has saved more than USD 100 billion, equivalent to Rs 9.4 lakh crore, on coal and natural gas imports from 2011 to 2025 using renewable energy, as stated by the International Energy Agency. Without added solar, wind, and bioenergy capacity since 2010, India would have needed around 110 million tonnes of additional imported coal in 2025, according to the International Energy Agency. Ferrexpo temporarily stopped production at its Ukrainian iron ore operations after recent attacks disrupted the country's electricity infrastructure. Ferrexpo reported that damage to substations and transmission lines made electricity supplies unstable and insufficient for maintaining production. Ferrexpo stated that its mining and processing assets escaped physical damage during the attacks.

Ferrexpo plans to resume production once commercial electricity supplies are restored, though no specific timetable was given. The recent stoppage marks Ferrexpo's third suspension of operations during 2026 due to power disruptions, limited working capital, and constrained export routes. Ferrexpo recorded commercial production of 780,000 tonnes in the September quarter, which was 19% lower than the 963,000 tonnes produced in the June quarter.
